</details>


### [236] [Context-Aware Disentanglement for Cross-Domain Sequential Recommendation: A Causal View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07992)
*Xingzi Wang,Qingtian Bian,Hui Fang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CoDiS，一个基于因果视角的跨域序列推荐(CDSR)框架，通过上下文感知的解耦方法解决现有方法忽略上下文混淆、梯度冲突和用户重叠假设三大问题，在三个真实数据集上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域序列推荐方法存在三大局限：忽视用户交互序列中不同上下文导致的虚假相关性、领域共享与领域特定偏好学习中的梯度冲突（ seesaw effect）、以及对大量用户重叠的不现实假设。这些问题限制了跨域知识迁移的有效性。

Method: CoDiS框架包含三个核心模块：(1) 变分上下文调整方法（Variational Context Adjustment）降低上下文的混淆效应；(2) 专家隔离与选择策略（Expert Isolation and Selection）解决梯度冲突问题；(3) 变分对抗解耦模块（Variational Adversarial Disentangling）彻底分离领域共享与领域特定表示。

Result: 在三个真实数据集上的广泛实验表明，CoDiS在统计显著性上一致优于现有的CDSR基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过因果视角和上下文感知的解耦框架，CoDiS有效解决了跨域序列推荐中的上下文混淆、梯度冲突和用户重叠依赖问题，验证了方法的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: Cross-Domain Sequential Recommendation (CDSR) aims to en-hance recommendation quality by transferring knowledge across domains, offering effective solutions to data sparsity and cold-start issues. However, existing methods face three major limitations: (1) they overlook varying contexts in user interaction sequences, resulting in spurious correlations that obscure the true causal relationships driving user preferences; (2) the learning of domain- shared and domain-specific preferences is hindered by gradient conflicts between domains, leading to a seesaw effect where performance in one domain improves at the expense of the other; (3) most methods rely on the unrealistic assumption of substantial user overlap across domains. To address these issues, we propose CoDiS, a context-aware disentanglement framework grounded in a causal view to accurately disentangle domain-shared and domain-specific preferences. Specifically, Our approach includes a variational context adjustment method to reduce confounding effects of contexts, expert isolation and selection strategies to resolve gradient conflict, and a variational adversarial disentangling module for the thorough disentanglement of domain-shared and domain-specific representations. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate that CoDiS consistently outperforms state-of-the-art CDSR baselines with statistical significance. Code is available at:https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CoDiS-6FA0.

</details>


### [237] [Beyond Dense Connectivity: Explicit Sparsity for Scalable Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08011)
*Yantao Yu,Sen Qiao,Lei Shen,Bing Wang,Xiaoyi Zeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对推荐系统中简单堆叠深度模型导致的性能瓶颈问题，提出SSR框架，通过显式稀疏架构（静态随机过滤+迭代竞争稀疏机制）实现"先过滤后融合"的多视图机制，在三个公开数据集和阿里全球电商十亿级工业数据集上验证了优于SOTA基线的性能，且具有更好的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推荐系统追求增大模型深度和容量以处理海量行为数据，但推荐数据高维稀疏，直接堆叠MLP等密集架构往往收效甚微甚至性能下降。分析发现工业CTR模型存在"隐式连接稀疏性"现象：大部分连接权重趋于零，说明密集架构与稀疏推荐数据之间存在结构不匹配，模型被迫处理大量低效用连接成为主要瓶颈。

Method: 提出SSR（Explicit Sparsity for Scalable Recommendation）框架，采用多视图"先过滤后融合"机制：1）将输入分解为并行视图；2）进行维度级稀疏过滤；3）执行密集融合。具体通过两种策略实现稀疏性：静态随机过滤器（Static Random Filter）通过固定维度子集实现高效结构稀疏；迭代竞争稀疏（ICS）采用生物启发的竞争机制自适应保留高响应维度。

Result: 在三个公开数据集和AliExpress十亿级工业数据集上的实验表明，SSR在相同参数量预算下优于SOTA基线。特别地，SSR展现出卓越的可扩展性——在密集模型性能趋于饱和时仍能持续提升。

Conclusion: 稀疏性应被显式嵌入模型架构而非仅依赖正则化隐式产生。SSR通过结构化稀疏设计有效弥合了密集架构与稀疏数据之间的鸿沟，为大规模推荐系统的可扩展性提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent progress in scaling large models has motivated recommender systems to increase model depth and capacity to better leverage massive behavioral data. However, recommendation inputs are high-dimensional and extremely sparse, and simply scaling dense backbones (e.g., deep MLPs) often yields diminishing returns or even performance degradation. Our analysis of industrial CTR models reveals a phenomenon of implicit connection sparsity: most learned connection weights tend towards zero, while only a small fraction remain prominent. This indicates a structural mismatch between dense connectivity and sparse recommendation data; by compelling the model to process vast low-utility connections instead of valid signals, the dense architecture itself becomes the primary bottleneck to effective pattern modeling. We propose \textbf{SSR} (Explicit \textbf{S}parsity for \textbf{S}calable \textbf{R}ecommendation), a framework that incorporates sparsity explicitly into the architecture. SSR employs a multi-view "filter-then-fuse" mechanism, decomposing inputs into parallel views for dimension-level sparse filtering followed by dense fusion. Specifically, we realize the sparsity via two strategies: a Static Random Filter that achieves efficient structural sparsity via fixed dimension subsets, and Iterative Competitive Sparse (ICS), a differentiable dynamic mechanism that employs bio-inspired competition to adaptively retain high-response dimensions. Experiments on three public datasets and a billion-scale industrial dataset from AliExpress (a global e-commerce platform) show that SSR outperforms state-of-the-art baselines under similar budgets. Crucially, SSR exhibits superior scalability, delivering continuous performance gains where dense models saturate.

</details>


### [238] [Search Changes Consumers' Minds: How Recognizing Gaps Drives Sustainable Choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08079)
*Frans van der Sluis,Leif Azzopardi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究探讨信息搜索如何弥合消费者负责任购物意愿与实际行为之间的差距。通过308名参与者的任务型研究，发现主动搜索伦理信息确实提升了消费者对伦理维度的重视程度，但真正驱动行为改变的是对伦理考量问题的认知和理解，而非单纯的搜索活动。意识到自身知识缺口并主动填补的参与者表现出显著的行为改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费者虽有负责任购物的意愿，但难以将其转化为实际行为。既往研究表明信息搜寻是影响因素之一，但具体机制尚不明确。

Method: 任务型实验研究，308名参与者针对正在选购的商品，从八个伦理维度中选择一个进行信息搜索。

Result: 主动搜索伦理信息提升了参与者对伦理维度的重视程度；然而，真正驱动购买决策向更负责任方向转变的是对伦理问题的认知与理解，而非伦理意图或搜索活动本身；意识到并填补知识空白的参与者表现出更强的行为改变意愿。

Conclusion: 负责任消费可视为部分信息问题，认识到自身知识局限可能是推动消费者行为实质性改变的关键催化剂。

Abstract: Despite a growing desire among consumers to shop responsibly, translating this intention into behaviour remains challenging. Previous work has identified that information seeking (or lack thereof) is a contributing factor to this intention-behaviour gap.In this paper, we hypothesize that searching can bridge this gap - helping consumers to make purchasing decisions that are better aligned with their values. We conducted a task-based study with 308 participants, asking them to search for information on one of eight ethical aspects regarding a product they were actively shopping for. Our findings show that actively searching for such information led to an overall increase in the importance participants' assigned to ethical aspects.However, it was the recognition and understanding of ethical considerations, rather than ethical intentions or search activity, that drove shifts towards more responsible purchasing decisions. Participants who acknowledged and filled knowledge gaps in their decision making showed significant behaviour change, including increased searching and a stronger desire to alter their future shopping habits. We conclude that responsible consumption can be considered a partial information problem, where awareness of one's own knowledge limitations may be the catalyst needed for meaningful consumer behaviour change.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [239] [Blink: CPU-Free LLM Inference by Delegating the Serving Stack to GPU and SmartNIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07609)
*Mohammad Siavashi,Mariano Scazzariello,Gerald Q. Maguire,Dejan Kostić,Marco Chiesa*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Blink是一种端到端LLM推理服务架构，通过将请求处理卸载到SmartNIC、利用RDMA直接将输入传递到GPU内存，并用持久GPU内核替代主机驱动的调度，从而将主机CPU从稳态推理路径中移除。该方案在CPU干扰下仍能保持稳定性能，而现有系统性能会下降达两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理正成为数据中心核心服务，但当前服务架构让主机CPU处于关键路径上进行编排和token级控制，导致LLM性能对CPU干扰敏感，削弱了应用共置效果，迫使运营商预留CPU余量，造成大量资源闲置。

Method: Blink将职责重新分配到SmartNIC和GPU：1) 将请求处理卸载到SmartNIC；2) 通过RDMA将输入直接传递到GPU内存；3) 用持久GPU内核替代主机驱动调度，实现批处理、调度和KV-cache管理，无需CPU参与。

Result: 对比TensorRT-LLM、vLLM和SGLang，Blink在独立运行时就已优于所有基线：P99 TTFT降低8.47倍，P99 TPOT降低3.40倍，decode吞吐量提升2.1倍，每token能耗降低48.6%。在CPU干扰下，Blink保持性能稳定，而现有系统性能下降高达两个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过将CPU从推理关键路径移除，Blink实现了更高的性能、更低的能耗以及对CPU干扰的免疫，为LLM推理服务提供了更高效、更稳定的基础设施方案。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) inference is rapidly becoming a core datacenter service, yet current serving stacks keep the host CPU on the critical path for orchestration and token-level control. This makes LLM performance sensitive to CPU interference, undermining application colocation and forcing operators to reserve CPU headroom, leaving substantial capacity unutilized.
  We introduce Blink, an end-to-end serving architecture that removes the host CPU from the steady-state inference path by redistributing responsibilities across a SmartNIC and a GPU. Blink offloads request handling to the SmartNIC, which delivers inputs directly into GPU memory via RDMA, and replaces host-driven scheduling with a persistent GPU kernel that performs batching, scheduling, and KV-cache management without CPU involvement.
  Evaluated against TensorRT-LLM, vLLM, and SGLang, Blink outperforms all baselines even in isolation, reducing pre-saturation P99 TTFT by up to 8.47$\times$ and P99 TPOT by up to 3.40$\times$, improving decode throughput by up to 2.1$\times$, and reducing energy per token by up to 48.6$\%$. Under CPU interference, Blink maintains stable performance, while existing systems degrade by up to two orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [240] [Reduced-Mass Orbital AI Inference via Integrated Solar, Compute, and Radiator Panels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07760)
*Stephen Gaalema,Samuel Indyk,Clinton Staley*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向太阳同步轨道（SSO）计算卫星的分布式计算架构，通过将光伏电池、散热器和计算功能集成到多个小型面板的大阵列中，实现>100 kW计算功率/吨的比功率密度，并可支持大规模LLM推理任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着太空计算需求的增长，现有卫星计算架构的比功率（<100 W/kg）和散热能力有限，制约了计算性能的提升。研究者旨在开发一种可扩展、高效能的太空计算基础设施。

Method: 1. 采用大面积蒸汽腔散热器设计，使芯片结温可控制在40°C左右；2. 将太阳能电池、散热器与计算单元集成于同一面板结构；3. 开发1-4 m²可扩展面板单元，支持面板间通信；4. 设计512面板子阵列架构以承载LLM推理负载。

Result: 实现约500 W/kg的比功率（现有方案<100 W/kg）；单星（16 MW计算/150吨）可支持31个512面板子阵列；同时运行>7900个LLM推理任务（500k token上下文窗口，553 tokens/sec/session）；单艘Starship可运输16 MW/150吨卫星。

Conclusion: 该架构通过热、电、计算三功能的空间共布局设计，突破了太空计算的热管理和质量约束瓶颈，具备从单艘火箭运输到在轨组装的逐级扩展能力，为未来太空数据中心和分布式计算星座奠定基础。

Abstract: We describe and analyze a distributed compute architecture for SSO computational satellites that can potentially provide >100 kW compute power per launched metric ton (including deployment and station keeping mass). The architecture co-locates and integrates the solar cells, radiator, and compute functions into multiple small panels arranged in a large array. The resultant large vapor chamber radiator area per panel should permit ICs to operate at junction temperatures near 40*C with benefits in compute efficiency and reliability. Using the structure of the radiator to support the solar cells may also yield a specific power of about 500 W/kg compared to less than 100 for existing conventional implementations. Assuming development of custom solutions for all components, a 16 MW computation, 150 ton satellite comprising a 20 m x 2200 m grid of 16,000 panels can fit in a single Starship hold. The concept is scalable to much larger satellites with higher mass payloads or using on-orbit assembly. We consider panel sizes from 1 to 4 m2 to allow trading vapor chamber heat transport with compute efficiency and inter-panel communication. Assuming a 1 kW/panel design, 512-panel subarrays of the satellite can run a representative inference-only LLM with 500,000 token context window and 128 attention blocks, at a rate of 553 tokens/sec/session, across 256 simultaneous in-flight sessions. A full satellite could support 31 such subarrays, for >7900 inferences at a time.

</details>


### [241] [LegoDiffusion: Micro-Serving Text-to-Image Diffusion Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08123)
*Lingyun Yang,Suyi Li,Tianyu Feng,Xiaoxiao Jiang,Zhipeng Di,Weiyi Lu,Kan Liu,Yinghao Yu,Tao Lan,Guodong Yang,Lin Qu,Liping Zhang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LegoDiffusion 是一个将扩散模型工作流分解为松耦合模型执行节点的系统，实现独立管理和调度，解锁集群级优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型服务系统将整个工作流视为黑盒整体进行资源配置和调度，存在以下问题：内部数据流不透明、模型无法共享、资源管理粒度过粗。

Method: 提出 LegoDiffusion 系统，将扩散模型工作流分解为可独立管理和调度的松耦合模型执行节点，实现显式管理单个模型推理，从而解锁以下优化：按模型伸缩、模型共享、自适应模型并行。

Result: LegoDiffusion 在性能上显著优于现有扩散模型工作流服务系统，可持续处理请求率提升最高 3 倍，容忍突发流量能力提升最高 8 倍。

Conclusion: 微服务化扩散模型工作流是可行且高效的架构方向，通过细粒度的模型管理可实现显著的集群资源优化和性能提升。

Abstract: Text-to-image generation executes a diffusion workflow comprising multiple models centered on a base diffusion model. Existing serving systems treat each workflow as an opaque monolith, provisioning, placing, and scaling all constituent models together, which obscures internal dataflow, prevents model sharing, and enforces coarse-grained resource management. In this paper, we make a case for micro-serving diffusion workflows with LegoDiffusion, a system that decomposes a workflow into loosely coupled model-execution nodes that can be independently managed and scheduled. By explicitly managing individual model inference, LegoDiffusion unlocks cluster-scale optimizations, including per-model scaling, model sharing, and adaptive model parallelism. Collectively, LegoDiffusion outperforms existing diffusion workflow serving systems, sustaining up to 3x higher request rates and tolerating up to 8x higher burst traffic.

</details>


### [242] [Wattlytics: A Web Platform for Co-Optimizing Performance, Energy, and TCO in HPC Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08182)
*Ayesha Afzal,Georg Hager,Gerhard Wellein*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Wattlytics是一个基于浏览器的交互式GPU系统决策支持工具，首创性地将基准测试驱动的性能扩展、DVFS感知功耗建模和多年度TCO分析集成于统一平台，支持7种当代GPU架构和多种科学工作负载，帮助用户在预算、GPU数量、性能和功耗约束下进行设计空间探索和敏感性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU加速计算系统的计算需求和能耗成本不断增长，使得系统设计者和运营者难以做出明智的决策。现有工具多为采购导向的简单计算器，缺乏对性能扩展、动态电压频率调节(DVFS)感知功耗建模以及多年度总拥有成本(TCO)的综合分析能力，导致用户难以在异构系统架构中进行全面的设计空间探索和成本效益评估。

Method: 该系统采用基准测试驱动的GPU性能扩展模型，结合DVFS感知的分段功耗建模方法，并集成了多维度决策指标计算（总拥有成本分解、每TCO工作量、每TCO功耗、每瓦每TCO工作量）。系统支持异构系统配置（涵盖GH200、H100、L40S、L40、A40、A100、L4等7种GPU架构），可选配代表性科学工作负载（GROMACS、AMBER等），并在约束条件下探索部署策略。此外，系统还提供了敏感性分析能力（弹性分析、Sobol指数、蒙特卡洛模拟）和协作功能。

Result: 案例研究在四种约束场景（固定预算、固定GPU数量、固定性能、固定功耗）下验证了系统的有效性。结果表明，在预算或能耗约束条件下，经过最优部署的高能效GPU在整体成本效益上可以优于高性能替代方案。

Conclusion: Wattlytics能够有效帮助用户探索设计参数空间，区分成本驱动因素和风险驱动因素，将HPC系统设计转化为可解释的明智决策过程。该工具通过整合性能、功耗和成本的综合分析，为集群设计和采购提供了科学依据。

Abstract: The escalating computational demands and energy footprint of GPU-accelerated computing systems complicate informed design and operational decisions. We present the first release of Wattlytics (https://wattlytics.netlify.app), an interactive, browser-based decision-support system. Unlike existing procurement-oriented calculators, Wattlytics uniquely integrates benchmark-driven GPU performance scaling, dynamic voltage and frequency scaling (DVFS)-aware piecewise power modeling, and multi-year total cost of ownership (TCO) analysis within a single interactive environment. Users can configure heterogeneous systems across contemporary GPU architectures (GH200, H100, L40S, L40, A40, A100, and L4), select representative scientific workloads (e.g., GROMACS, AMBER), and explore deployment scenarios under constraints such as energy prices, system lifetime, and frequency scaling. Wattlytics computes multidimensional decision metrics (TCO breakdown, work-per-TCO, power-per-TCO, and work-per-watt-per-TCO) and supports design-space exploration, what-if scenarios, sensitivity metrics (elasticity, Sobol indices, Monte Carlo) and collaborative features to guide realistic cluster design and procurement under uncertainty. We demonstrate selected scenarios comparing deployment strategies under different operational modes: ixed budget, fixed GPU count, fixed performance, and fixed power. Our case studies show that, under budget or energy constraints, optimally deployed energy-efficient GPUs can outperform higher-performance alternatives in overall cost-effectiveness. Wattlytics helps users explore the design parameter space and distinguish between cost- and risk-driving factors, turning HPC design into a well-informed and explainable decision-making process.

</details>


### [243] [Asynchronous Quantum Distributed Computing: Causality, Snapshots, and Global Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08298)
*Siddhartha Visveswara Jayanti,Anand Natarajan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 研究异步量子分布式系统，提出基于经典Chandy-Lamport算法的量子分布式算法(QGO)来实现可分解的全局量子操作，证明了Lamport计算因果性在量子世界仍然有效，并给出了量子分布式计算的形式化模型和规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠缠使得系统无法简单地通过全局量子态描述，这给分布式系统中的因果性分析带来挑战。现有经典分布式算法（如Chandy-Lamport快照算法）是否能推广到量子领域尚不明确。

Method: 基于经典Chandy-Lamport快照算法，设计量子分布式算法(QGO)实现可分解的全局量子操作；通过分析量子纠缠对系统描述的影响，验证Lamport计算因果性框架在量子世界中的适用性。

Result: 成功实现量子分布式全局操作算法；证明即使在存在量子纠缠（无法用全局量子态简单描述）的情况下，Lamport的计算因果性论证在量子世界中仍然有效。

Conclusion: 量子分布式计算可基于经典分布式算法的思想进行设计；计算因果性是量子与经典分布式系统间的共同理论基础；提出的形式化模型和规范方法对随机算法等经典场景也有参考价值。

Abstract: We initiate the study of asynchronous quantum distributed systems, focusing on the case of implementing atomic quantum global operations that can be decomposed into a collection of local operations on the components of the system. A simple example of such an operation is a quantum snapshot in which the whole system is instantaneously measured. Based on the classical snapshot algorithm of Chandy and Lamport, we design a quantum distributed algorithm to implement such decomposable global operations, which we call the QGO Algorithm. The analysis of our algorithm shows that arguments based on Lamport's computational causality remain valid in the quantum world, even though, due to entanglement, causality is not manifest from the standard description of the system in terms of a (global) quantum state. Our other contributions include a formal model of quantum distributed computing, and a formal specification for the desired behavior of a global operation, which may be of interest even in classical settings (such as in the setting of randomized algorithms).

</details>


### [244] [City-Scale Visibility Graph Analysis via GPU-Accelerated HyperBall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08374)
*Alex Hodge,Melissa Barrientos Trinanes*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合三种技术的系统，将Visibility Graph Analysis (VGA)扩展到城市规模：delta-compressed CSR存储（LEB128编码实现~4x压缩）、HyperBall概率距离估算（首次应用于可见性图）以及GPU加速CUDA内核。在42,705个单元格上实现239倍端到端加速，并能处理236,000个单元格（48亿条边）仅需137秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VGA依赖全对BFS计算，实际应用被限制在小规模研究区域，难以满足城市级空间分析需求。

Method: (1) Delta压缩CSR存储+LEB128变长整数编码，约4倍压缩率，支持超出RAM的内存映射图；(2) HyperBall概率距离估算器（基于HyperLogLog计数器传播），将BFS复杂度从O(N|E|)降至O(D|E|2^p)，首次应用于可见性图；(3) GPU CUDA融合解码联合内核，共享内存内完成LEB128解码并通过PCIe流式传输压缩图。

Result: 相比depthmapX实现239倍端到端加速（42,705单元格），可扩展至236,000单元格（48亿边）仅需137秒。p=10时Visual Mean Depth达Pearson r=0.999，中位相对误差1.7%。

Conclusion: 该系统使VGA首次能够处理城市级规模问题，性能远超现有工具，同时保持较高的估算精度。

Abstract: Visibility Graph Analysis (VGA) is a key space syntax method for understanding how spatial configuration shapes human movement, but its reliance on all-pairs BFS computation limits practical application to small study areas. We present a system that combines three techniques to scale VGA to city-scale problems: (i) delta-compressed CSR storage using LEB128 varint encoding, which achieves ~4x compression and enables memory-mapped graphs exceeding available RAM; (ii) HyperBall, a probabilistic distance estimator based on HyperLogLog counter propagation, applied here for the first time to visibility graphs, reducing BFS complexity from O(N|E|) to O(D|E|2^p); and (iii) GPU-accelerated CUDA kernels with a fused decode-union kernel that streams the compressed graph via PCIe and performs LEB128 decoding entirely in shared memory. HyperBall's iteration count equals the topological depth limit, so the radius-n analysis that practitioners already use as standard translates directly into proportional speedup -- unlike depthmapX, whose BFS time is invariant to depth setting due to the small diameter of visibility graphs. Using depthmapX's own visibility algorithm (sparkSieve2) to ensure identical edge sets, our tool achieves a 239x end-to-end speedup at 42,705 cells and scales to 236,000 cells (4.8 billion edges) in 137 seconds -- problem sizes far beyond depthmapX's practical limit. At p=10, Visual Mean Depth achieves Pearson r=0.999 with 1.7% median relative error across 20 matched configurations.

</details>


### [245] [Taming GPU Underutilization via Static Partitioning and Fine-grained CPU Offloading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08451)
*Gabin Schieffer,Ruimin Shi,Jie Ren,Ivy Peng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对多实例GPU（MIG）技术进行了系统级特性分析，研究其对HPC工作负载的支持能力。实验覆盖科学计算（NelRS、LAMMPS）、AI（Llama3）和量子计算（Qiskit）等多种应用，发现MIG虽能提升资源利用率和能效，但共享资源干扰问题依然存在，且粗粒度资源分配与应用需求存在不匹配。针对此问题，提出利用Nvlink-C2C互连的内存卸载方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前GPU资源利用率普遍偏低，多实例GPU（MIG）通过将GPU计算和内存资源划分为固定大小的切片并提供隔离，被视为提升利用率的有前景方案，但其对HPC工作负载的有效性和局限性尚不明确。

Method: 采用真实应用（NelRS、LAMMPS、Llama3、Qiskit）进行系统级表征分析，涵盖科学计算、AI和数据 analytics 工作负载；评估指标包括资源利用率、性能扩展、能效和干扰效应。

Result: 研究表明：1）MIG可显著减少资源浪费，提升系统吞吐量和能效；2）通过共享资源（如电源管理）仍会产生干扰；3）粗粒度的计算/内存资源配置常与应用需求不匹配。

Conclusion: 提出基于缓存一致性Nvlink-C2C互连的内存卸载方案，旨在弥合粗粒度资源切片与应用实际需求之间的差距，减少资源浪费。

Abstract: Advances in GPU compute throughput and memory capacity brings significant opportunities to a wide range of workloads. However, efficiently utilizing these resources remains challenging, particularly because diverse application characteristics may result in imbalanced utilization. Multi-Instance GPU (MIG) is a promising approach to improve utilization by partitioning GPU compute and memory resources into fixed-size slices with isolation. Yet, its effectiveness and limitations in supporting HPC workloads remain an open question. We present a comprehensive system-level characterization of different GPU sharing options using real-world scientific, AI, and data analytics applications, including NekRS, LAMMPS, Llama3, and Qiskit. Our analysis reveals that while GPU sharing via MIG can significantly reduce resource underutilization, and enable system-level improvements in throughput and energy, interference still occurs through shared resources, such as power throttling. Our performance-resource scaling results indicate that coarse-grained provisioning for tightly coupled compute and memory resources often mismatches application needs. To address this mismatch, we propose a memory-offloading scheme that leverages the cache-coherent Nvlink-C2C interconnect to bridge the gap between coarse-grained resource slices and reduce resource underutilization.

</details>
